# agentflow.computation.proof-report.model@2

Status: normative, registered non-wire semantics. Registered is not Supported or Activated. R3 producers, T4 normalizer, G02 full lossless Scope, independent full oracle and T8 activation are NOT IMPLEMENTED/NOT AUTHORIZED by this staging. No AFIDL, ABI, ACE1 grammar or HashV1 domain changes.

## Closed descriptor language and canonical identity

This single JSON block materializes the complete reachable logical descriptor graph,not a runtime reflection plan. Named refs must resolve;records have exactly every ordered field;enums/variants have closed full payloads;options use explicit none:null/some tags;sets/maps retain full typed key/value semantics. Native lifetimes/borrows denote owned logical content,not addresses. Imported frozen declarations remain complete and source-bound. All successor field/type/rank identities are distinct from unchanged old editions.

ACE1 is unchanged:record fields emit unsigned UTF8 name order;enums use their exact names and variants single-case objects;option None is {"none":null},Some is {"some":payload},never bare null. Numerics keep nominal widths and exact values;2 precedes10 by numeric ordering,not text. NFC and raw UTF8-byte claim positions are distinct;bytes use existing canonical byte form. Sets reject duplicate complete identity/conflicting payload;sequences preserve declared order. Only identical complete origins may unite observations in the future normalizer.

Canonical value DAGs are finite,minimal,reachable,acyclic and bottom-up interned by complete typed values,ranked by height/type/nonrecursive payload/ordered children. No rank/hash-only shortcut replaces semantic typed equality. Scope excludes fact/obligation/report/capability descendants and has no recursive digest equation. All count/depth/work/output limits intersect existing earlier bounds;overflow/resource excess fails with no partial success or capability. Encoding,normalization,complete-scope binding and witness production are not implemented by descriptor registration or metadata shape validation.

of complete key/value records with unique typed keys","Node":"uint64 canonical value-DAG rank of the exact declared model type","Opt":"explicit none:unit or some:T; missing is not None","Raw":"recursively replace untrusted text positions with exact UTF8 bytes, retain native enum/option/numeric types and all sequence order","Seq":"ordered semantic sequence, duplicate occurrences retained","Set":"unique complete typed values in typed order; conflicting equal keys rejected","imports":"complete frozen declarations below are normative logical shape input; storage references flatten to owned logical values, never native addresses","nominal_ids":"distinct uint32 Computation/Expression/RunParameter/ValueSlot/ResourceSlot/Port/ContractRef; ordinals/indices checked uint64","record":"all named fields required, no extra fields; written declaration order for semantic comparison","unknown":"reject unknown type, field, variant, width, role, clause or edition; no reflection fallback","variant":"one closed case and its complete payload; case declaration order supplies rank"},"obligation_edition":"agentflow.g01.obligation@1","required_types":["Scope","FactKey","Fact","ObligationIdentity","Obligation","PrerequisiteKey","EvidenceRef","TypedContradiction","ObservationSite","Source","Target","Coordinate","Requirement","CanonicalValueDag"],"scope_excluded_descendants":["Fact","Obligation","Scope","CompleteView","final_report","CheckedRootExportCompatibility"],"semantic_constraints":["Untrusted assertion descriptors use the deterministic **Raw(T)** companion family: transform each text/string/identifier/digest-spelling scalar position into exact UTF8 bytes, preserving its original nominal field/model tag; keep actual byte fields as bytes, fixed-width native numerics as their exact nominal widths, option tags and actual native enum cases; recursively transform every field/case payload; preserve every Vec as sequence, including bad assertion order. Native set values retain their defined set semantics. Freeze the complete resulting companion descriptors in RM, not a runtime reflection transform. Thus Raw(CompilerClaim),Raw(Context),Raw(binding) retain invalid/NFD compiler labels,paths,pins and names without normalizing them. Checked owner/source subjects use ordinary validated descriptors. Comparing a claimed text scalar to the owner compares exact UTF8 bytes, not raw/checked wrapper tags or normalized text. A truly unknown native model case still fails UnsupportedModel; unknown transport cases fail earlier. This family gives byte-exact claim preservation without an opaque serialized object or a natural-language parser.","Kind ranks1–25, exactly: assertion_input,exact_pin,proof_context,resolver_provenance,compiler_identity,subflow_dependency,input_mapping,output_producer,failure_containment,effect_containment,resource_mapping,information_flow,usage_coverage,recovery_coverage,execution_source,execution_feasibility,execution_security,body_conformance,implementation_conformance,scope_authority,scope_budget,runtime_authority,publisher_authentication,control_evidence,prerequisite.","Dimension = none(rank0), I1,O2,Phi3,epsilon4,R5,F6,U7,K8,X9, or supporting{predicate:PredicateId}. Supporting cases compare after root dimensions by the closed predicate case text. Role ranks follow section2; they are derived and need not appear in Identity again.","Requirement={clause:RequirementClause,evidence_class:EvidenceClass}.\nRequirementClause is a closed tagged sum direct:DirectKind | recovery:RecoveryClause | control:ControlClause | prerequisite:PrerequisitePredicate. DirectKind is precisely kinds1–13 and15–23, in kind-rank order. The other three payload enums are the following ordered lists. The enclosing kind must agree with the clause family and direct payload. This explicit tag avoids conflating identically named control/direct clauses in a context-free codec.\nFor kinds1–13 and15–23, clause is that kind's tag. Recovery14 clauses in order: source_closure,action_binding,usage_profile,multiplicity,guard_compatibility,epsilon_coverage,recovery_usage_coverage,eligibility,commit_order.\nControl24: resolved_leaf,topology,output_interface,selector_totality,effect_envelope,failure_row,resource_provenance,information_flow,usage,retry_safety,child_settlement,forward_history,compensation_coverage,root_export_boundary,unsupported_control,recovery_blocked_outcome.\nPrerequisite25: closed_contract_schema,imported_module_closure,schema_universe,positive_unique_ports,failure_pins,effect_operation_membership,resource_universe,effect_resource_relation,flow_catalogs,io_resource_namespaces,additive_meter_units,recovery_usage_exhaustiveness,closed_effect_action_coverage,exact_k_identity,execution_universes,flow_execution_relation,required_dimensions,exact_relation_pins.","EvidenceClass ranks: static_declaration,typed_counterexample_resolution,trusted_body,implementation_attestation,provider_receipt,authority_attestation,publisher_authentication,control_terminal_state,committed_effect_order,recovery_outcome,provider_honoring,application_idempotency. Ordinary violations KEEP the predicate's evidence class; they do not automatically become typed_counterexample_resolution.","Allowed class relation is predicate-specific, not merely clause-wide: static predicates use static_declaration; body/implementation use trusted_body/implementation_attestation; runtime/publisher use authority_attestation/publisher_authentication; Scope authority/budget static_declaration with Unproven; actual owned-child settlement uses control_terminal_state, while static timeout-outcome separation and passive-AWAIT declarations use static_declaration; commit_order uses committed_effect_order; recovery_blocked_outcome uses recovery_outcome; compensation eligibility uses provider_receipt; retry facts distinguish static declarations, provider_honoring and application_idempotency predicates by their actual evidence query. Counterexample_resolution is only an explicitly declared prerequisite asking to resolve a typed contradiction. No unsupported cross-product is accepted.","PredicateFamily = root_preflight | root_dimension | execution_child | root_boundary | leaf | control | root_recovery | prerequisite. ClosedPredicateCase:\n- root_preflight: assertion_input,exact_pin,proof_context,compiler_identity,subflow_dependency.\n- root_dimension: I,O,Phi,epsilon,R,F,U,K,X.\n- execution_child: source,feasibility,security.\n- root_boundary: body,implementation,scope_authority,scope_budget,runtime,publisher; subflow-body is leaf/body_conformance under its exact call path.\n- prerequisite: the18 clauses above plus conditional-applicability queries epsilon_nonempty and flow_constraints_require_relation (support predicates, not new arbitrary missing categories).\n- leaf/control/root_recovery: the exact cases in closed_predicates; dynamic wrappers keep the original PredicateId, not the wrapper function name. Each map row binds one of the closed clauses above and its frozen governing RuleRef. No site line number is a runtime case.\nThe RM roster must include both successful and non-successful branch variants and their mandatory source prerequisites; proof aggregation priority is never a substitute for this binding.","Compiler={analysis_semantics_version:Text,registry_content:Bytes,rule_registry_content:Bytes,specifications:Seq<SpecificationContentIdentity>,rules:Seq<RuleContentIdentity>,support_manifest:FullSupportManifest}; each SpecificationContentIdentity={specification_id,logical_path,content_sha256,content:Bytes}; each RuleContentIdentity={rule_id,normative_source,source_anchor,short_statement}. These vectors retain original assertion sequence order when untrusted; owner-generated projections use their declared canonical order. All bytes, including complete registry rows not repeated in the four-field rule projection, are retained.","FullSupportManifest={normative_sources:Set<SpecificationContentIdentity>,closed_tables:{hash_domains:Bytes,abi_numbers:Bytes,rule_areas:Bytes,afidl_primitives:Bytes},selected_specifications:Set<SpecificationId>,applied_rules:Set<RuleContentIdentity>,semantic_roles:Seq<SemanticRoleEntry>,attribution:AttributionProfile,report_shape:ModelEdition,obligation_edition:ObligationEdition}. SemanticRoleEntry and AttributionProfile reuse the exact full existing Portable AFIDL types, including all role/dependency/status fields; only the full_content_revalidation case is supported. The owner has all46 source bindings here,20 selected specifications and138 applied rules as specified in the edition plan. This complete support view is recaptured from existing Portable manifest fields/implementation-owned source bytes; it is a new non-wire report model field, NOT a new AFIDL field or a hidden 46->20 content reduction. Owner support is data built from frozen sources, not a report-dependent self-checksum.","CompilerClaim is the closed sum legacy_v1{analysis_semantics_version,registry_content,rule_registry_content,specifications,rules with their original exact types} | successor_v2:Compiler. Assertion input descriptors use Raw(CompilerClaim), rather than demanding a stale v1 claim grow an invented support_manifest or canonical NFC labels. The original full old claim can therefore yield a new-owner compiler-preflight mismatch with its bytes intact. Scope's owner compiler must be successor_v2 and exact supported; these are different tagged comparison operands.","Context={compiled_modules:Map<SpecificationId,CompiledModuleContents>,type_schemas:Map<LogicalTypeIdentity,CanonicalTypeSchema>,effect_catalogs:Map<SpecificationId,Set<Text>>,resource_types:{schemas:Map<LogicalTypeIdentity,CanonicalTypeSchema>,descriptors:Map<LogicalTypeIdentity,ResourceTypeDescriptor>},flow_catalogs:{confidentiality:Set<ConfidentialityLabelRef>,taints:Set<TaintRef>,sinks:Map<EffectOperationRef,Bool>},usage_meters:Map<UsageMeterRef,{meter:UsageMeterRef,unit:UsageUnitRef,aggregation:UsageAggregation}>,recovery_operations:{effects:Map<SpecificationId,Set<Text>>,reconciliation_operations:Set<{catalog:Text,name:Text}>},execution_universes:ExecutionUniverseContents,relation_snapshots:Seq<RelationSnapshotContent>,resolver_dependencies:ResolverDependencyManifest}.","CompiledModuleContents={specification_id,types:Seq<CanonicalTypeSchema>}; CanonicalTypeSchema uses the exact complete TypeSchema machine grammar in AFIDL1 §§6–7, including nominal identities, field/case/value numbers and full type expressions. It is not just TypeSchemaRef. ResourceTypeDescriptor={specification_id,type_name,schema_hex,recognized_attributes:Set<Text>}. ExecutionUniverseContents={capabilities:Map<ExecutionCapabilityRef,{capability,exact_version:Text,supported_versions:Set<Text>,attributes:Set<{key:Text,value:Text}>}>,trust_domains:Set<TrustDomainRef>,regions:Set<RegionRef>,region_compatibility:Set<{from:RegionRef,to:RegionRef}>,runtime_profiles:Map<RuntimeProfileRef,{profile,family:Text,features:Set<Text>}>}. Preserve empty recognized universes and direction of compatibility edges.","RelationSnapshotContent={pin:{schema:TypeSchemaRef,revision:Text,canonical_content_checksum:{algorithm:Text,hex:Text}},canonical_content:Bytes}. ResolverDependencyManifest={component_contract_digests:Seq<DigestRef>,subflow_targets:Seq<{semantic_ir_digest:DigestRef,direct_dependencies:Seq<DigestRef>,exported_contract_digest:Opt<DigestRef>}>}. ResolverAssociations adds exact caller_ir,source_node/request and checked target/export association, complete component/target content, direct/transitive dependencies; its per-association record is {caller_ir,source_node,request:Request,component_contract:Opt<Node<CheckedContractContents>>,target_ir:Opt<Node<CheckedIrContents>>,target_export:ExportState,direct_dependencies:Set<DigestRef>,transitive_dependencies:Set<DigestRef>}. Assert the resolved variant's required fields; no arbitrary all-None resolved row.","RootProvenance={source_ir:DigestRef,root_node:ComputationId,root_resolver_identity:Node<ResolverAssociations>,root_context:Node<Context>,root_k_context:Node<Context>,source_ir_contents:Node<CheckedIrContents>}. CheckedContractContents and CheckedIrContents contain the complete existing ComponentContract/SemanticIrDocument values AND exact canonical input bytes/pins, not a Checked wrapper serialized as authority. RawCandidateContract uses the same named-field descriptor but retains raw claims/order before validation.","AssertionGraph nodes have all fields of Raw(RootExportAssertion) (root_export.rs317–329), all Raw(binding) records (183–313) with their full declared widths/options, and all recursively nested target assertion identities. Seven mapping vectors and dependency vectors are sequences; invalid order is not repaired. Full RootInputUseSite, structural type AST, flow mapping partition/bindings, action/source/usage profiles, execution requirements and transitive targets use their exact source-defined logical models with Raw companions at untrusted text positions. All imported AFIDL payloads are full source-pinned values. A serializer containing only a count, checksum, StructuralType display string without an invertible declared grammar, or omitted universe is not this model.","ContextPartition=Seq<NonemptySeq<ContextRole>>: each applicable role exactly once; members sorted numeric; classes sorted minimum. Root roles analysis0,checked_export1,snapshot2,root3,root_K4; contract roles analysis0,candidate1,old_checked2 iff present; computation roles analysis0,snapshot1,leaves2,root3,root_K4. Equal contents can occupy different classes. Never encode native addresses. This metadata is a comparison of already checked object associations, not evidence that supplied contents share an arena.","TypedValue={model_type:ModelType,value:CanonicalValueDag}, with exactly one root of that model_type, is a value under a **closed ModelType descriptor**, materialized as the following finite DAG. Its self-contained DAG is finite; it never contains Scope/Fact/Obligation/report as a descendant. ModelType is an exact member of RM's closure, bound to source edition/type name and full descriptor; no arbitrary string creates a new model.","ModelType={owner:SpecificationId,name:Text}: owner is an exact source-pinned imported AFIDL specification or RM@1; name must occur in its registered closed descriptor closure. Full descriptors are obtained from the complete owner/source bundle in Scope, not serialized recursively inside each ModelType. ClosedField/ClosedCase are exact text tokens present in that ModelType's approved ordered descriptor. Unknown owner/name/field/case is UnsupportedModel. This is type reference by full supported content, not an unbound local table index or arbitrary model-name string.","DagNode={type:ModelType,body:NodeBody}.\nNodeBody = scalar:ScalarValue\n | record:Seq<{field:ClosedField,value:DagRank}>\n | variant:{case:ClosedCase,payload:DagRank}\n | sequence:Seq<DagRank> | set:Seq<DagRank>\n | option_none:unit | option_some:DagRank.\nScalarValue = unit:unit | null:unit | bool:Bool | int32:I32 | uint32:U32\n | int64:I64 | uint64:U64 | decimal:ExactDecimal | float64:FiniteFloat64\n | string:NfcText | bytes:Bytes | timestamp:MicrosI64 | duration:MicrosI64.\nCanonicalValueDag={nodes:Seq<DagNode>,roots:NonemptySeq<DagRank>}.\nNode<T> is a typed DagRank whose descriptor is exactly T. Roots are in ScopeSubject field order, duplicates allowed for equal-valued roots. Value nodes cannot contain Fact/Obligation/report/Scope as descendants. Identity's Scope is a complete graph; every used member/evidence node must be reachable from its subject roots. Object graphs that need extra evidence values add them as declared input roots in their registered subject, never as final-report roots.","Validate acyclicity, widths/descriptors and semantic sets first. Intern equal typed values bottom-up (no hash-only equality); node height0 for scalars/empty containers, otherwise1+maximum child height. Sort each height by descriptor type identity then nonrecursive payload and ordered child ranks, assign contiguous ranks starting0; child ranks strictly smaller. Sets sort by their domain's typed key/full payload; sequence order is preserved. Require no duplicates,unreachable nodes,alternate node numbering or redundant equal nodes at decode. A duplicated equal subtree and shared subtree yield the same minimal DAG. No recursion through final report or digest fixed point exists.","Identity order is scope,assertion_path,stage,derived role,dimension,kind,source,source_ordinal,mapping_ordinal,target,coordinate,rule,requirement,origin. Ordinals are repeated from validated Origin/Source solely for this key and MUST agree. Edition precedes Scope for differing editions (normalizers admit one edition only). Source/Target/Coordinate/Evidence/contradiction/observation variants use declaration ranks; other records field order above; Requirement clauses use kind-family rank then listed clause order. Rule order=(specification_id,rule_id,anchor). Scalar types are nominal; mathematical numeric comparison, exact rational Decimal, finite float after frozen zero canonicalization, bool false<true, unsigned UTF8/bytes, None<Some, lexicographic tuple/sequence prefix first. Map/set compare sorted complete logical values; DAG ranks are canonical representation, **not a shortcut for semantic numeric order**. Comparisons memoize typed node pairs and charge work.","Set uniqueness: FactKey/PrerequisiteKey by complete key; obligations by complete identity; evidence/observation/contradictions by complete typed value. Conflicting key payload rejects. Semantic sequences preserve duplicates. Unknown fields/cases/type descriptors/role/domain combinations reject; new additions require new model/obligation/analysis editions under0214/0221, not an ignore-unknown assertion descriptors use the deterministic **Raw(T)** companion family: transform each text/string/identifier/digest-spelling scalar position into exact UTF8 bytes, preserving its original nominal field/model tag; keep actual byte fields as bytes, fixed-width native numerics as their exact nominal widths, option tags and actual native enum cases; recursively transform every field/case payload; preserve every Vec as sequence, including bad assertion order. Native set values retain their defined set semantics. Freeze the complete resulting companion descriptors in RM, not a runtime reflection transform. Thus Raw(CompilerClaim),Raw(Context),Raw(binding) retain invalid/NFD compiler labels,paths,pins and names without normalizing them. Checked owner/source subjects use ordinary validated descriptors. Comparing a claimed text scalar to the owner compares exact UTF8 bytes, not raw/checked wrapper tags or normalized text. A truly unknown native model case still fails UnsupportedModel; unknown transport cases fail earlier. This explicit tag avoids conflating identically named control/direct clauses in a context-free codec.\nFor kinds1–13 and15–23, clause is that kind's tag. Recovery14 clauses in order: source_closure,action_binding,usage_profile,multiplicity,guard_compatibility,epsilon_coverage,recovery_usage_coverage,eligibility,commit_order.\nControl24: resolved_leaf,topology,output_interface,selector_totality,effect_envelope,failure_row,resource_provenance,information_flow,usage,retry_safety,child_settlement,forward_history,compensation_coverage,root_export_boundary,unsupported_control,recovery_blocked_outcome.\nPrerequisite25: closed_contract_schema,imported_module_closure,schema_universe,positive_unique_ports,failure_pins,effect_operation_membership,resource_universe,effect_resource_relation,flow_catalogs,io_resource_namespaces,additive_meter_units,recovery_usage_exhaustiveness,closed_effect_action_coverage,exact_k_identity,execution_universes,flow_execution_relation,required_dimensions,exact_relation_pins.","EvidenceClass ranks: static_declaration,typed_counterexample_resolution,trusted_body,implementation_attestation,provider_receipt,authority_attestation,publisher_authentication,control_terminal_state,committed_effect_order,recovery_outcome,provider_honoring,application_idempotency. Roots are in ScopeSubject field order, duplicates allowed for equal-valued roots. Value nodes cannot contain Fact/Obligation/report/Scope as descendants. Identity's Scope is a complete graph; every used member/evidence node must be reachable from its subject roots. Object graphs that need extra evidence values add them as declared input roots in their registered subject, never as final-report roots.","Validate acyclicity, widths/descriptors and semantic sets first. Sets sort by their domain's typed key/full payload; sequence order is preserved. Require no duplicates,unreachable nodes,alternate node numbering or redundant equal nodes at decode. A duplicated equal subtree and shared subtree yield the same minimal DAG. Rule order=(specification_id,rule_id,anchor). <a id="successor-descriptor-closure"></a>

## AF-SEMANTIC-0242

MUST: Every reachable successor descriptor has closed fields, variants, nominal widths, option presence, typed ordering and bounded DAG semantics; unknown types reject and no runtime reflection or new ACE1 grammar is permitted.

Direct normative dependencies: AF-SEMANTIC-0214, AF-SEMANTIC-0215, AF-SEMANTIC-0216, AF-SEMANTIC-0217, AF-SEMANTIC-0218, AF-SEMANTIC-0221.

Registered/staged responsibility:g01-successor-staging/rule-0242;full producer/normalizer/oracle conformance remains R3/T4–T8,not executed by this reference.
